Worker reap admission measured evidence, 2026-10-14, macOS arm64.

Each run names its exact before/after subject manifests and raw log digest.
Manifests are deduplicated by SHA-256; all before/after pairs matched.
Sources include tracked project inputs, Cargo.lock, .cargo/config.toml and
the external measurement wrapper. Generated target artifacts, caches,
installed dependencies/tools and external runtime state are not frozen.
The fixed k12 task matrix and specs are included in each source manifest.
Exploratory fixture/scheduling/lint failures below are not credited green.
Mutants are intentional failures; no reported failure confirms physical reap.

Log encoding: every ASCII x run of >=256 characters is represented as
[[repeat-x:N]]. Expanding that marker to N x characters reconstructs the
original raw log exactly; every round trip was verified against its digest.
The notation preserves byte counts without pages of repeated policy output.

The last restored focused inputs exactly match workspace-final inputs.
All 35 C2 case names match item by item, not merely by totals.

Measurement wrapper:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'
label=$1
shift
out=".jj/k12-measure/$label"
mkdir "$out"
jj file list > "$out/paths"
# Cargo configuration is intentionally absent from graph indexing, but read by cargo.
if ! rg -q '^\.cargo/config.toml$' "$out/paths"; then
  printf '%s\n' .cargo/config.toml >> "$out/paths"
fi
LC_ALL=C sort -u "$out/paths" -o "$out/paths"
snapshot() {
  local path
  while IFS= read -r path; do
    if [[ -f "$path" ]]; then shasum -a 256 "$path"; else printf 'MISSING  %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done < "$out/paths"
  shasum -a 256 /tmp/ravel-k12-measure.sh
}
snapshot > "$out/before.sha256"
printf '%q ' "$@" > "$out/command"
printf '\n' >> "$out/command"
set +e
"$@" > "$out/log" 2>&1
status=$?
set -e
snapshot > "$out/after.sha256"
printf '%s\n' "$status" > "$out/status"
cmp "$out/before.sha256" "$out/after.sha256"
printf 'measurement=%s status=%s subjects=%s immutable=true\n' "$label" "$status" "$(wc -l < "$out/before.sha256")"
tail -n 18 "$out/log"
exit "$status"

profile.<name>.threads-required

- Type: Integer or string
- Description: Number of threads each test reserves from the pool.
- Documentation: Heavy tests and threads-required
  <https://nexte.st/docs/configuration/threads-required/>
- Valid values: Positive integer, "num-cpus", or "num-test-threads"
- Default: 1

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

SOURCES = [Path(p) for p in ['docs/components.md', 'docs/specs/result-job-completion.md',
    'docs/completion-failure-design-evidence.md',
    'docs/adr/policy-evaluation-precedes-the-launch.md']]
subjects: set[Path] = set(SOURCES)


def check_link(source: Path, link: str) -> None:
    parsed = urlsplit(link)
    if parsed.scheme or parsed.netloc:
        return
    target = (source.parent / unquote(parsed.path)).resolve() if parsed.path else source.resolve()
    assert target.is_file(), f'missing file: {source} -> {link}'
    subjects.add(target)
    if not parsed.fragment:
        return
    text = target.read_text()
    fragment = unquote(parsed.fragment)
    if re.fullmatch(r'L\d+', fragment):
        assert 0 < int(fragment[1:]) <= len(text.splitlines()), f'bad line: {link}'
    elif target.suffix == '.md':
        headings = re.findall(r'^#{1,6}\s+(.+?)\s*$', text, re.M)
        anchors = {re.sub(r'[^\w -]', '', h.lower()).replace(' ', '-') for h in headings}
        assert fragment in anchors, f'missing anchor: {source} -> {link}'
    elif target.suffix == '.html':
        anchors = set(re.findall(r'id="([^"\n]+)"', text))
        manifest = target.parent / 'diagrams.json'
        if manifest.exists():
            subjects.add(manifest)
            data = json.loads(manifest.read_text())
            assert "section.id = 'diagram-' + item.id" in text
            anchors.update('diagram-' + d['id'] for d in data['diagrams'])
        assert fragment in anchors, f'missing HTML anchor: {source} -> {link}'
    else:
        raise AssertionError(f'unchecked anchor type: {link}')


count = 0
for source in SOURCES:
    for link in re.findall(r'\[[^\]]*\]\(([^\s)]+)\)', source.read_text()):
        check_link(source, link)
        count += 1
for link in ['intentionally-missing-k12.md', 'components.md#intentionally-missing-k12']:
    try:
        check_link(Path('docs/components.md'), link)
    except AssertionError as error:
        print(f'negative control rejected: {error}')
    else:
        raise AssertionError(f'broken validator accepted {link}')
for file in sorted(subjects):
    if file.is_file():
        print(hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), file)
print(f'validated {count} links; source/target subjects listed above; HTML dynamic IDs checked against source and manifest, no rendering claim')
Path('.jj/k12-measure/doc-subjects.json').write_text(json.dumps([str(p.resolve().relative_to(Path.cwd())) for p in subjects if p.is_file()]))
